i < node.length; i++) {\n       visitNode(node[i], fn, node, i);\n     }\n     return;\n   }\n \n   if (typeof node.type !== \"string\") {\n     return;\n   }\n \n   for (const key of Object.keys(node)) {\n     visitNode(node[key], fn, node, key);\n   }\n \n   const replacement = fn(node);\n \n   if (replacement) {\n     parent[property] = replacement;\n   }\n }\n \n function isUnbalancedLogicalTree(node) {\n   return (\n     node.type === \"LogicalExpression\" &&\n     node.right.type === \"LogicalExpression\" &&\n     node.operator === node.right.operator\n   );\n }\n \n function rebalanceLogicalTree(node) {\n   if (!isUnbalancedLogicalTree(node)) {\n     return node;\n   }\n \n   return rebalanceLogicalTree({\n     type: \"LogicalExpression\",\n     operator: node.operator,\n     left: rebalanceLogicalTree({\n       type: \"LogicalExpression\",\n       operator: node.operator,\n       left: node.left,\n       right: node.right.left,\n       ...composeLoc(node.left, node.right.left),\n     }),\n     right: node.right.right,\n     ...composeLoc(node),\n   });\n }\n \n module.exports = postprocess;\n \n"},"toolTelemetry":{"properties":{"command":"view","options":"{\"truncateBasedOn\":\"tokenCount\",\"truncateStyle\":\"middle\"}","inputs":"[\"path\",\"command\"]","resolvedPathAgainstCwd":"false","fileExtension":"[\".js\"]","viewType":"file"},"metrics":{"resultLength":5877,"resultForLlmLength":5877,"responseTokenLimit":32000},"restrictedProperties":{}}},"id":"2ea1ad0a-29eb-402e-a667-ed0e2a8474bd","timestamp":"2026-07-01T09:47:51.668Z","parentId":"3cd5e1b4-3a1b-411b-a18d-8c78f0143ad0"}
